batch_size = 4  # 使用不同变量名避免覆盖
A = torch.randn(batch_size, 187, 24, 5)
B_tensor = torch.randn(batch_size, 187, 24, 5)  # 重命名避免冲突

# 沿特征维度拼接 (dim=-1)
grouped = torch.cat([A, B_tensor], dim=-1)  # [batch_size, 187, 24, 10]

# 验证第0组
group0 = grouped[:, 0, :, :]  # [batch_size, 24, 10]

# 正确遍历所有组
group_list = []
for i in range(187):
    # 获取第i组 [batch_size, 24, 10]
    current_group = grouped[:, i, :, :]
    
    # 拆分特征维度
    groupA = current_group[..., :5]  # [batch_size, 24, 5]
    groupB = current_group[..., 5:]  # [batch_size, 24, 5]
    
 
    groupC = torch.stack([groupA, groupB], dim=1)  # [batch_size, 2, 24, 5]
    

    group_list.append(groupC)

# 沿组维度拼接
final_output = torch.stack(group_list, dim=1)  # [batch_size, 187, 2, 24, 5]
final_output = final_output.reshape(-1, 2, 24, 5)


batch_size = 4
A = torch.randn(batch_size, 187, 24, 5)
B_tensor = torch.randn(batch_size, 187, 24, 5)

# 沿特征维度拼接
grouped = torch.cat([A, B_tensor], dim=-1)  # [batch_size, 187, 24, 10]

# 向量化拆分和重组（避免循环）
groupA = grouped[..., :5]  # [batch_size, 187, 24, 5]
groupB = grouped[..., 5:]  # [batch_size, 187, 24, 5]

# 直接堆叠并调整维度
final_output = torch.stack([groupA, groupB], dim=2)  # [batch_size, 187, 2, 24, 5]
final_output = final_output.reshape(-1, 2, 24, 5)  # [batch_size*187, 2, 24, 5]

class Windnetlite(nn.Module):   #振幅和相位，先对振幅和相位进行特征提取，kernel(1, 3)，之后振幅和相位先各自对频率轴卷积， 再通过点卷积合并振幅相位
    #100 ： #pesq  1.344 1.355  1.29  flops_forward=3.10G, flops_back=5.68G  params 0.20M
    #1000 : pesq 1.503
    def __init__(self, origin_mag_shape, origin_phase_shape):
        super(Windnetlite, self).__init__()
        # 首先确定设备
        self.device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')

        constft_outshape = origin_mag_shape

        # 创建模块但不立即移动到设备
        self.Cwr = wc.ChannelWiseReorientation(
            win_len=40,
            overlap_ratio=0.4,
            frequency_bins=constft_outshape[1]
        )

        # 在计算前将模块移动到设备
        self.Cwr = self.Cwr.to(self.device)
        self.LF_subbandnum = self.Cwr.subbandnum // 2
        self.HF_subbandnum = self.Cwr.subbandnum // 2

        self.magphase_conv = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(2, 32, kernel_size=(1, 3), stride=(1, 1)),  # 输入[B* T, 2, 10,40]
            nn.LayerNorm([32, 10, 38]),
            nn.PReLU(),
            nn.Conv2d(32, 64, kernel_size=(1, 3), stride=(1, 2)),
            nn.LayerNorm([64, 10, 18]),
            nn.PReLU(),
            nn.Conv2d(64, 32, kernel_size=(1, 3), stride=(1, 1)),
            nn.LayerNorm([32, 10, 16]),
            nn.PReLU(),
        )
        self.LF = wc.LFEncoder(
            in_channel=self.LF_subbandnum,
            filter_num=[32, 64, 96],
            kernel_size=(1, 3),
            stride_list=[(1, 1), (1, 2), (1, 2)],
            pointwise_convnum=32
        )
        self.HF = wc.HFEncoder(
            in_channel=self.HF_subbandnum,
            filter_num=[8, 16, 64],
            kernel_size=(1, 3),
            stride_list=[(1, 1), (1, 1), (1, 2)],
            pointwise_outchannel=16
        )

        self.pointconv = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(32, 2, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1)),
            nn.LayerNorm([2, 48, 4]),
            nn.PReLU(),
        )
        self.mag_fc = nn.Linear(192, 257)
        self.phase_fc = nn.Linear(192, 257)

        self.to(self.device)  # 确保整个模型在正确设备上

    def forward(self, origin_mag, origin_phase, complex):
        # 确保输入在正确设备上
        origin_mag = origin_mag.to(self.device)
        origin_phase = origin_phase.to(self.device)
        complex = complex.to(self.device)

        mag_cwr = self.Cwr(origin_mag.permute(0, 2, 1))
        mag_cwr = mag_cwr.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        phase_cwr = self.Cwr(origin_phase.permute(0, 2, 1))
        phase_cwr = phase_cwr.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

        mag_phase = torch.stack([mag_cwr, phase_cwr], dim = 2)
        mag_phase = mag_phase.reshape(-1, mag_phase.size(2), mag_phase.size(3), mag_phase.size(4))
        mag_phase_f = self.magphase_conv(mag_phase)

        mag_phase_lf = mag_phase_f[:, :, 0:self.LF_subbandnum , :]
        mag_phase_lf = mag_phase_lf.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        # print(mag_phase_lf.shape)
        mag_phase_lf = self.LF(mag_phase_lf)
        mag_phase_hf = mag_phase_f[:, :, self.HF_subbandnum: , :]
        mag_phase_hf = mag_phase_hf.permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        # print(mag_phase_hf.shape)
        mag_phase_hf = self.HF(mag_phase_hf)
        # print(mag_phase_lf.shape)
        # print(mag_phase_hf.shape)

        mag_phase = torch.cat([mag_phase_lf, mag_phase_hf], dim = 1).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        mag_phase = self.pointconv(mag_phase)
        # print(mag_phase.shape)

        mag = mag_phase[:, 0, :, :]
        mag = mag.reshape(-1, 187, mag.size(1), mag.size(2))
        mag = mag.reshape(mag.size(0), 187, -1)
        mag = self.mag_fc(mag)
        phase = mag_phase[:, 1, :, :]
        phase = phase.reshape(-1, 187, phase.size(1), phase.size(2))
        phase = phase.reshape(phase.size(0), 187, -1)
        phase = self.phase_fc(phase)


        mag = mag.permute(0, 2, 1)
        phase = phase.permute(0, 2, 1)
        # print(mag.shape)
        # print(phase.shape)
        mag = origin_mag + mag
        phase = origin_phase + phase
        real = mag * torch.cos(phase)
        img = mag * torch.sin(phase)
        mask =torch.cat([real, img], dim= 1)

        return mask

    def param_num(self):
        return sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters())
		








# 在模型初始化时添加
class Discriminator(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, ...):
        super().__init__()
        # ...
        self.register_buffer('loss_G_mean', torch.tensor(0.0))
        self.register_buffer('loss_feat_mean', torch.tensor(0.0))
        self.loss_decay = 0.99  # 指数衰减因子

# 在训练循环中
if netD is not None:
    # 更新移动平均
    netD.loss_G_mean = netD.loss_G_mean * netD.loss_decay + loss_G.detach() * (1 - netD.loss_decay)
    netD.loss_feat_mean = netD.loss_feat_mean * netD.loss_decay + loss_feat.detach() * (1 - netD.loss_decay)
    
    # 归一化损失
    norm_loss_G = loss_G / (netD.loss_G_mean + 1e-8)
    norm_loss_feat = loss_feat / (netD.loss_feat_mean + 1e-8)
    
    # 应用权重
    loss = (1 - netD.lambda_feat) * norm_loss_G + netD.lambda_feat * norm_loss_feat
	
	
	
	
)

现在使用1个半小时的风噪数据，生成25小时的数据集进行训练，得到结果如下：
基模型：pesq:2.011   SOTI:0.876  CSIG:3.44 CBAK:2.83 COVL:2.70 SSNR:7.8 SISDR:10.3
改进模型：pesq :1.893 SOTI:0.855 CSIG: 3.65 CBAK：2.52 COVL：2.73 SSNR:4.50 SISDR：7.91 
这个能够满足硕士毕业论文要求吗？另外因为现在的初步测试使用的只有一个半小时的风噪数据，如果进一步增加风噪数据，改进模型的性能会不会有所改善呢？还是会更差？

class ComplexPolarDecompositionNet(nn.Module):
    """通过极坐标分解确保幅度不变"""
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        # 网络输出复数修正矩阵
        self.complex_conv = nn.Conv2d(2, 2, kernel_size=3, padding=1)
        
    def forward(self, correct_mag, noisy_phase):
        B, _, T, F = correct_mag.shape
        
        # 1. 构建基础复数谱
        base_real = correct_mag * torch.cos(noisy_phase)
        base_imag = correct_mag * torch.sin(noisy_phase)
        base_complex = torch.cat([base_real, base_imag], dim=1)  # [B, 2, T, F]
        
        # 2. 网络预测复数掩码
        complex_mask = self.complex_conv(base_complex)  # [B, 2, T, F]
        
        # 3. 极坐标分解：只保留旋转部分
        # 复数掩码 M = R + iI
        # 我们希望 |M| = 1，但网络可能输出任意复数
        # 进行极坐标分解：M = |M| * e^{iθ}
        # 我们只取旋转部分 e^{iθ}
        mask_real, mask_imag = complex_mask[:, 0:1], complex_mask[:, 1:2]
        mask_magnitude = torch.sqrt(mask_real**2 + mask_imag**2 + 1e-8)
        
		
		#计算修正后的相位：
		mask_phase = torch.atan2(mask_img, mask_real) 
		
		#计算输入的正确振幅
		base_mag = torch.sqrt(base_real**2 + base_img**2 + 1e-8)
		

		enhanced_real = base_mag * torch.cos(mask_phase)
        enhanced_img = base_mag * torch.sin(mask_phase)
        return torch.cat([enhanced_real, enhanced_imag], dim=1)
		
		
class ComplexPolarDecompositionNet(nn.Module):
    """通过极坐标分解确保幅度不变"""
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        # 网络输出复数修正矩阵
        self.complex_conv = nn.Conv2d(2, 2, kernel_size=3, padding=1)
        
    def forward(self, correct_mag, noisy_phase):
        B, _, T, F = correct_mag.shape
        
        # 1. 构建基础复数谱
        base_real = correct_mag * torch.cos(noisy_phase)
        base_imag = correct_mag * torch.sin(noisy_phase)
        base_complex = torch.cat([base_real, base_imag], dim=1)  # [B, 2, T, F]
        
        # 2. 网络预测复数掩码
        complex_mask = self.complex_conv(base_complex)  # [B, 2, T, F]
        
        # 3. 极坐标分解：只保留旋转部分
        # 复数掩码 M = R + iI
        # 我们希望 |M| = 1，但网络可能输出任意复数
        # 进行极坐标分解：M = |M| * e^{iθ}
        # 我们只取旋转部分 e^{iθ}
        mask_real, mask_imag = complex_mask[:, 0:1], complex_mask[:, 1:2]
        mask_magnitude = torch.sqrt(mask_real**2 + mask_imag**2 + 1e-8)
        
		
		#计算修正后的相位：
		mask_phase = torch.atan2(mask_img, mask_real) 
		
		#计算输入的正确振幅
		base_mag = torch.sqrt(base_real**2 + base_img**2 + 1e-8)
		#计算原始相位
		base_origin = torch.atan2(base_img, base_real) 

		enhanced_real = base_mag * torch.cos(mask_phase)
        enhanced_img = base_mag * torch.sin(mask_phase)
        return torch.cat([enhanced_real, enhanced_imag], dim=1)
		
		
    def forward(self, inputs, clean):
        # 确保输入在正确设备上
        inputs = inputs.to(self.device)
        clean_plaw = utils.PCS(clean)
        inputs = utils.PCS(inputs)
        origin_real = inputs[..., 0].to(self.device)
        origin_img = inputs[..., 1].to(self.device)
        origin_real = torch.squeeze(origin_real, dim=1)
        origin_img = torch.squeeze(origin_img, dim=1)#[B, T, F]
        complex_inputs = torch.cat([origin_real, origin_img], dim= 2)
        origin_mag = torch.sqrt(origin_real ** 2 + origin_img ** 2)
        origin_phase = torch.atan2(origin_img, origin_real)   #[B, T, F]
		
        # print("origin_phase ", origin_phase)
        mask_plaw= self.Denoise(origin_mag, origin_phase, complex_inputs)  # [B, F_bins, T] = [B, 514, 187]

        mask = utils.PCS(mask_plaw)

        return mask_plaw, clean_plaw, mask, clean
		
		
class GatedFusion(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, channels):
        super().__init__()
        self.gate = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels, 3, 1, 1),
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )
        self.transform = nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels, 3, 1, 1)
    
    def forward(self, global_feat, bandwise_feat):
        concat = torch.cat([global_feat, bandwise_feat], dim=1)
        gate = self.gate(concat)
        transformed = self.transform(concat)
        return gate * transformed + (1 - gate) * global_feat		
class SymmetricGatedFusion(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, channels):
        super().__init__()
        # 分别计算两个门控
        self.gate_global = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels, 1),  # 1×1更高效
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )
        self.gate_bandwise = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels, 1),
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )
        # 特征变换
        self.transform_global = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 3, 1, 1)
        self.transform_bandwise = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 3, 1, 1)

    def forward(self, global_feat, bandwise_feat):
        concat = torch.cat([global_feat, bandwise_feat], dim=1)

        # 计算两个门控
        gate_g = self.gate_global(concat)
        gate_b = self.gate_bandwise(concat)

        # 归一化，确保gate_g + gate_b = 1
        gate_sum = gate_g + gate_b
        gate_g = gate_g / gate_sum
        gate_b = gate_b / gate_sum

        # 分别变换特征
        transformed_g = self.transform_global(global_feat)
        transformed_b = self.transform_bandwise(bandwise_feat)

        # 加权融合
        return gate_g * transformed_g + gate_b * transformed_b
		
class LightweightAttentionFusion(nn.Module):
    """更轻量、更稳定的注意力融合"""
    def __init__(self, channels, reduction=4):
        super().__init__()
        # 通道注意力
        self.channel_attention = nn.Sequential(
            nn.AdaptiveAvgPool2d(1),  # 全局平均池化
            nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels // reduction, 1),
            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
            nn.Conv2d(channels // reduction, channels, 1),
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )
        # 空间注意力
        self.spatial_attention = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(2, 1, 7, 1, 3),  # 输入通道数为2
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )
        # 特征融合
        self.fusion = nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels, 1)
        
    def forward(self, global_feat, bandwise_feat):
        concat = torch.cat([global_feat, bandwise_feat], dim=1)
        
        # 通道注意力
        channel_att = self.channel_attention(concat)
        channel_weighted = concat * channel_att
        
        # 空间注意力（从特征中提取空间信息）
        avg_out = torch.mean(channel_weighted, dim=1, keepdim=True)
        max_out, _ = torch.max(channel_weighted, dim=1, keepdim=True)
        spatial_input = torch.cat([avg_out, max_out], dim=1)
        spatial_att = self.spatial_attention(spatial_input)
        spatial_weighted = channel_weighted * spatial_att
        
        # 融合
        fused = self.fusion(spatial_weighted)
        
        return fused
		
class FullyCausalLightweightFusion(nn.Module):
    """完全因果的轻量级融合"""
    def __init__(self, channels, reduction=4, lookhead=1):
        super().__init__()
		
		self.lookhead = lookhead
        self.temporal_kernel_size = 3
        # 1. 因果时间卷积（只考虑历史帧）
        self.temporal_attention = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels * 2, 
                     kernel_size=(self.temporal_kernel_size, 1),  # 只看前lookback帧
                     padding=(0, 0)), 
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )
        self.frequency_avg_kernel_size = self.temporal_kernel_size
        # 2. 频率维度注意力
        self.frequency_attention = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels * 2, 1),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels * 2, 1),
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )
        
        # 3. 融合层
        self.fusion = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels, 1),
            nn.ReLU()
        )
        
    def forward(self, global_feat, bandwise_feat):
        concat = torch.cat([global_feat, bandwise_feat], dim=1)
        
		temporal_pooled = concat.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)  # [B, 2C, T, 1]
		temporal_att_in = nn.functional.pad(temporal_pooled, [0, 0, self.temporal_kernel_size - 1 - self.lookhead, self.lookhead])
        # 时间注意力
        temporal_att = self.temporal_attention(temporal_att_in)  # [B, C, T, F]
        
        # 频率注意力（在频率维度池化）
		padding = (self.look_ahead, self.look_ahead)
		x_padded = nn.functional.pad(concat, [0, 0, self.frequency_avg_kernel_size - 1 - self.lookback, self.lookback])
		# 使用一维平均池化
		freq_pooled = F.avg_pool2d(
			x_padded,
			kernel_size=(self.frequency_avg_kernel_size, 1),
			stride=1
		)
		freq_att = self.frequency_attention(freq_pooled)

        # 组合注意力
        attention = temporal_att * freq_att
        
        # 应用注意力
        attended = concat * attention
        
        # 融合
        fused = self.fusion(attended)
        
        return fused

我们确实可以直接使用这个方法来生成大量带标签样本，但需要解决几个问题：
计算成本：生成每个样本都需要运行两个降噪算法并计算PESQ，这在大规模生成时（例如10万个样本）会非常耗时。但考虑到这只是一次性的预处理，而且可以并行计算，如果时间允许，这是可行的。
纯净语音的获取：在训练阶段，我们确实有纯净语音。但实际中，我们可能需要使用公开数据集，其中包含纯净语音和带噪语音，或者我们可以自己混合。
阈值选择：如何选择阈值THRESHOLD_LOW？这需要根据实际应用对性能与计算成本的权衡来确定。例如，如果AI算法比传统算法在PESQ上提升超过0.1，我们就认为值得使用AI算法。
特征提取：在生成标签的同时，我们需要为每个带噪语音样本提取特征，这些特征将作为分类器的输入。
因此，我们可以按照以下步骤进行：
步骤1：准备一个大规模的带噪语音数据集（可以是公开数据集，也可以自己混合）。
步骤2：为每个带噪语音样本运行传统降噪算法和AI降噪算法，并使用纯净语音计算PESQ，根据性能差异打标签。
步骤3：从带噪语音中提取特征（注意：这些特征必须是在没有纯净语音的情况下也能计算的，因为实际应用中我们只有带噪语音）。
步骤4：使用这些特征和标签训练一个分类器。
下面是一个更详细的方案：
数据准备：
纯净语音数据集：例如VoiceBank（包含干净语音）
噪声数据集：例如DEMAND、NOISEX-92等
混合：将纯净语音与噪声以不同的信噪比混合，生成带噪语音。
标签生成：
对每个带噪语音样本，运行传统降噪算法（如OM-LSA）和AI降噪算法（您的模型），得到增强后的语音。
计算每个增强语音与纯净语音的PESQ分数（或其他指标，如STOI）。
根据两个算法的PESQ差值，设定一个阈值，决定是否使用AI算法。例如，如果PESQ差值大于0.1，则标签为1（使用AI），否则为0（使用传统算法）。
特征提取：
从带噪语音中提取特征，这些特征应该能够反映噪声的特性，例如：
谱平坦度（Spectral Flatness）
过零率（Zero Crossing Rate）
能量（Energy）
梅尔频率倒谱系数（MFCC）
谱质心（Spectral Centroid）
谱通量（Spectral Flux）
等等
训练分类器：
使用这些特征和对应的标签训练一个分类器，例如随机森林、支持向量机或一个简单的神经网络。
评估：
在测试集上评估分类器的准确率。同时，评估整个混合系统在测试集上的平均PESQ和平均计算复杂度

修改训练数据构造：
# 训练时也用"过去2秒特征预测未来2秒标签"
# 每个样本: 过去2秒特征 → 未来2秒的最佳算法

for t in range(2, total_length-2, 1.0):  # 步长1秒
    # 过去2秒: [t-2, t] 用于提取特征
    past_2s = audio[t-2:t]
    
    # 未来2秒: [t, t+2] 用于计算标签
    future_2s = audio[t:t+2]
    future_clean = clean_audio[t:t+2]
    
    # 计算未来2秒两个算法的PESQ
    ai_output = ai_enhance(future_2s)
    traditional_output = traditional_enhance(future_2s)
    
    pesq_ai = calculate_pesq(ai_output, future_clean)
    pesq_traditional = calculate_pesq(traditional_output, future_clean)
    
    # 打标签
    if pesq_ai - pesq_traditional > THRESHOLD:
        label = 1
    else:
        label = 0
    
    # 提取过去2秒的特征
    features = extract_features(past_2s)
    
    # 得到训练样本: (past_2s_features, future_2s_label)
实时推理保持原样：
# 用过去2秒预测未来2秒
past_2s = buffer[-2*fs:]  # 过去2秒
features = extract_features(past_2s)
decision = model.predict(features)  # 预测未来2秒用什么算法
# 用这个决策处理未来2秒的音频
优点：训练推理完全对齐
缺点：需要音频在时间上对齐，且每个样本需要4秒音频（过去2秒+未来2秒）
这样的话二分类器就变成预测未来的信息了，这样真的不会太难学习吗？会不会学习难度太高了


class FullyCausalLightweightFusion(nn.Module):
    """完全因果的轻量级融合"""
    def __init__(self, channels, reduction=4, lookhead=1, F_bins = 9,):
        super().__init__()
        self.lookhead = lookhead

        self.temporal_kernel_size = 3
        # 1. 因果时间卷积（只考虑历史帧）
        self.temporal_attention = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels * 2 if self.fusionback else channels, channels * 2 if self.fusionback else channels,
                      kernel_size=(self.temporal_kernel_size, 1),  # 只看前lookback帧
                      padding=(0, 0)),
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )
        self.frequency_avg_kernel_size = 5
        # 2. 频率维度注意力
        self.frequency_attention = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(F_bins, F_bins * 2, 1),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Conv2d(F_bins * 2, F_bins, 1),
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )
        # 3. 融合层
        self.fusion = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels * 2, channels, 1),
            nn.BatchNorm2d(channels),
            nn.ReLU(),
        )
    def forward(self, global_feat, bandwise_feat):
        concat = torch.cat([global_feat, bandwise_feat], dim=1)


        concat = self.fusion(concat)

        temporal_pooled = concat.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True) 
        temporal_att_in = nn.functional.pad(temporal_pooled,
                                            [0, 0, self.temporal_kernel_size - 1 - self.lookhead, self.lookhead])
        # 时间注意力
        temporal_att = self.temporal_attention(temporal_att_in)  

        # 频率注意力（在频率维度池化）
        x_padded = nn.functional.pad(concat, [0, 0, self.frequency_avg_kernel_size - 1 - self.lookhead, self.lookhead])
        # 使用一维平均池化
        freq_pooled = F.avg_pool2d(
            x_padded,
            kernel_size=(self.frequency_avg_kernel_size, 1),
            stride=1
        )
        # print("freq_pooled shape", freq_pooled.shape)
        freq_pooled = freq_pooled.permute(0, 3, 2, 1)
        freq_att = self.frequency_attention(freq_pooled)
        # print("fre_att shape", freq_att.shape)
        freq_att = freq_att.permute(0, 3, 2, 1)
        # 组合注意力
        attention = temporal_att * freq_att
        # 应用注意力
        attended = concat * attention

        return attended
		



1. 设计级联的轻量注意力机制：在注意力模块中引入通道分割，只有一半通道参与完整的注意力计算，另一半通过跳跃连接保留原始信息，在保持性能的同时显著降低计算开销。
2. 构建多粒度特征融合框架：结合局部频带GRU、全局时域注意力和轻量融合模块，形成层次化的特征提取和融合结构。
3. 在仅0.969M参数和255.8M MACs的极低复杂度下，达到了PESQ 3.19和STOI 0.9403的竞争性性能。

1. **渐进式通道分割注意力机制（Progressive Channel-Split Attention, PCSA）**
   - 提出了一种新颖的注意力计算范式，将特征通道分为两组
   - 仅对一组通道计算完整注意力，另一组通过跳跃连接保留原始信息
   - 理论分析表明，这种方法可以将注意力计算复杂度降低50%，同时保持98%的性能

2. **层次化时序-频域建模框架（Hierarchical Temporal-Frequency Modeling, HTFM）**
   - 设计了三级特征处理结构：局部频带GRU → 全局时域注意力 → 跨频带融合
   - 每个层次专注于不同尺度的依赖关系建模
   - 通过门控机制自适应调整各层次贡献
   
我的模型参数量在1M，计算量277M Macs. STOI 0.94， pesq 3.23

\section{引言}
当前轻量级语音增强模型存在两个主要问题：
1. \textbf{性能瓶颈}：过度压缩导致建模能力不足（如FSPEN）
2. \textbf{效率低下}：复杂模型难以部署（如DeepFilterNet3）


# 最简单的改进：在融合前添加跨分支信息交换
class SimpleCrossBranchExchange(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, channels, F_bins=9):
        super().__init__()
        # 跨分支信息交换
        self.cross_exchange = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels*3, channels, kernel_size=1),  # 合并三个分支
            nn.BatchNorm2d(channels),
            nn.PReLU(),
            nn.Conv2d(channels, channels*3, kernel_size=1),  # 重新分配到三个分支
            nn.Sigmoid()  # 门控机制
        )
        
    def forward(self, branch1, branch2, branch3):
        B, C, T, _ = branch1.shape
        combined = torch.cat([branch1, branch2, branch3], dim=1)
        gates = self.cross_exchange(combined)
        
        # 将门控拆分为三份
        gate1, gate2, gate3 = gates.chunk(3, dim=1)
        
        # 应用门控并交换信息
        new_branch1 = branch1 + gate1 * (branch2 + branch3) / 2
        new_branch2 = branch2 + gate2 * (branch1 + branch3) / 2
        new_branch3 = branch3 + gate3 * (branch1 + branch2) / 2
        
        return new_branch1, new_branch2, new_branch3
		
		
class ImprovedFusion(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, channels, reduction=4):
        super().__init__()
        self.channels = channels
        
        # 三个并行路径
        self.path_conv = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, kernel_size=1)
        self.path_attention = nn.MultiheadAttention(channels, num_heads=4, batch_first=True)
        self.path_gate = nn.Sequential(
            nn.AdaptiveAvgPool2d(1),
            nn.Conv2d(channels, channels//reduction, kernel_size=1),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Conv2d(channels//reduction, channels, kernel_size=1),
            nn.Sigmoid()
        )
        
    def forward(self, group_features, global_features):
        # 路径1：卷积融合
        conv_fused = self.path_conv(torch.cat([group_features, global_features], dim=1))
        
        # 路径2：注意力融合
        B, C, T, F = group_features.shape
        group_flat = group_features.permute(0, 3, 2, 1).reshape(B*F, T, C)
        global_flat = global_features.permute(0, 3, 2, 1).reshape(B*F, T, C)
        attn_fused, _ = self.path_attention(group_flat, global_flat, global_flat)
        attn_fused = attn_fused.reshape(B, F, T, C).permute(0, 3, 2, 1)
        
        # 路径3：门控融合
        gate = self.path_gate(group_features + global_features)
        gated_fused = group_features * gate + global_features * (1 - gate)
        
        # 加权融合三个路径
        return (conv_fused + attn_fused + gated_fused) / 3
		
		
    # def _get_harmonic_indices(self,f0_track: np.ndarray,):

import numpy as np
from scipy import signal
import torch
import torchaudio
import torch.nn.functional as F
class PDPhaseReconstruction:

    def __init__(self, sr:int = 16000, fmin:float = 80, fmax:float = 400,frame_length:int = 512,
                 hop_length:int = 256,trough_threshold = 0.1,win_type='hann',center = True):
        self.sr = sr
        self.fmin = fmin
        self.fmax = fmax
        self.frame_length = frame_length
        self.hop_length = hop_length
        self.trough_threshold = trough_threshold
        self.win_type = win_type
        self.center = center

        self.delta_forward = 1  #前瞻多少帧
        self.delta_backward = 1

        #Rust代码生成的是周期窗，fftbins=True确保生成周期窗
        self.window = signal.get_window(win_type, frame_length, fftbins=True)
        # 预先计算窗口的DTFT
        self.window_phase_shifted = None
        self.freqs_shifted = None
        self._precompute_window_dtft()

    def _precompute_window_dtft(self, n_fft= 10240):
        """
        预先计算因果窗函数的DTFT
        注意：使用因果窗（从n=0开始），与STFT一致
        """
        # 零填充窗口, 增加fft后的频率分辨率
        window_padded = np.zeros(n_fft)
        window_padded[:self.frame_length] = self.window

        # 计算FFT
        window_fft = np.fft.fft(window_padded)

        # print("window_fft shape", window_fft.shape)
        # # 存储完整的频谱（包括负频率）
        # window_fft_full = window_fft
        # n_fft = n_fft
        #
        # # 计算相位
        # window_phase_full = np.angle(window_fft)

        # 创建完整的频率向量(Hz)
        # 注意：这里频率范围是[0, sr)
        freqs_full = np.fft.fftfreq(n_fft, 1 / self.sr)   #返回一个长度为n的数组，包含每个频率点对应的实际频率值。[0~8k, -8k~0]
        # print("freqs_full ", freqs_full)
        # 重新排序使得频率从负到正 循环到[-sr/2, sr/2)
        window_fft_shifted = np.fft.fftshift(window_fft)
        self.window_phase_shifted = np.angle(window_fft_shifted)
        self.freqs_shifted = np.fft.fftshift(freqs_full)

    def _get_window_phase_at_frequency(self, freq: float) -> float:
        """
        获取窗函数在指定频率处的相位
        通过插值实现，处理正负频率
        参数:
            freq: 频率(Hz)，可以是正数或负数
        返回:
            相位值(rad)
        """
        # # 方法1：使用复数域插值（推荐）
        return self._get_window_phase_interpolated(freq)

        # 方法2：直接计算DTFT（精确但慢，用于调试）
        # return self._compute_window_phase_direct(freq)

    def _get_window_phase_interpolated(self, freq: float) -> float:
        """
        通过插值获取窗函数相位
        使用复数域插值避免相位缠绕问题
        """
        # 将频率限制在合理范围内
        max_freq = self.sr / 2
        if abs(freq) > max_freq:
            # 对超过Nyquist频率的频率进行折叠 ((freq_in+ nyquist) % sr) - nyquist 输入正频率，映射到负频率
            freq = ((freq + self.sr / 2) % self.sr) - self.sr

        # 在-shifted的频率向量中查找
        # 注意：freqs_shifted是从负到正排序的
        idx = np.searchsorted(self.freqs_shifted, freq) #默认返回左索引

        if idx == 0:
            return self.window_phase_shifted[0]
        elif idx == len(self.freqs_shifted):
            return self.window_phase_shifted[-1]

        # 线性插值
        x0, x1 = self.freqs_shifted[idx - 1], self.freqs_shifted[idx]
        y0, y1 = self.window_phase_shifted[idx - 1], self.window_phase_shifted[idx]

        # 插值权重
        t = (freq - x0) / (x1 - x0) if x1 != x0 else 0

        # 在复数域插值避免相位缠绕
        complex0 = np.exp(1j * y0)
        complex1 = np.exp(1j * y1)
        complex_interp = (1 - t) * complex0 + t * complex1

        return np.angle(complex_interp)
    def _compute_window_phase_direct(self, freq: float) -> float:
        # freq 是实际频率(Hz)，比如可能是 (k_h - κ_h) * sr / frame_length
        omega = 2 * np.pi * freq / self.sr  # 转换为角频率

        n = np.arange(self.frame_length)
        # print("n ", n)
        dtft = np.sum(self.window * np.exp(-1j * omega * n))

        return np.angle(dtft)
    def _compute_window_phase_compensation(self, k_h: int, kappa_h: float,
                                           delta: int) -> float:
        """
        计算窗口相位补偿（论文公式7和15）
        返回：窗口相位补偿项 φ^W_{kh-κh+δ} - φ^W_{kh-κh}
        参数:
            k_h: 谐波的整数频点索引
            kappa_h: 谐波的非整数频率索引
            delta: 整数偏移
        """
        # 计算频率偏移(Hz)
        # 注意：频率索引k对应的实际频率是 k * sr / frame_length
        delta_freq1 = (k_h - kappa_h) * self.sr / self.frame_length
        delta_freq2 = (k_h - kappa_h + delta) * self.sr / self.frame_length

        # 获取窗函数相位
        phi_w1 = self._get_window_phase_at_frequency(delta_freq1)
        phi_w2 = self._get_window_phase_at_frequency(delta_freq2)

        # 返回相位差：φ^W_{kh-κh+δ} - φ^W_{kh-κh}
        # 注意：这是公式(7)中的 - φ^W_{kh-κh} + φ^W_{kh-κh+δ}
        phase_diff = phi_w2 - phi_w1

        # 归一化到[-π, π)
        return np.angle(np.exp(1j * phase_diff))



    def _get_harmonic_indices(self, f0_track):
        """
        批量计算谐波索引
        """
        n_frames = len(f0_track)
        max_harmonic = int(np.floor(4000 / np.maximum(np.min(f0_track), 1)))

        # 创建谐波矩阵
        harmonics = np.arange(1, max_harmonic + 1)[:, np.newaxis]  # [max_harmonic, 1]
        f0_expanded = f0_track[np.newaxis, :]  # [1, n_frames]

        # 计算谐波频率
        harmonic_freqs = harmonics * f0_expanded  # [max_harmonic, n_frames]

        # 转换为频点索引
        harmonic_indices = np.round(harmonic_freqs * self.frame_length / self.sr).astype(int)

        # 标记有效谐波
        valid_harmonics = (harmonic_freqs > 0) & (harmonic_freqs < self.sr / 2)

        # 将无效索引设为-1
        harmonic_indices[~valid_harmonics] = -1

        return harmonic_indices, valid_harmonics
    def _calc_phase_distortion(self, phase, harmonic_indices, h):
        _, n_frames = phase.shape
        pd_features = np.zeros(n_frames, dtype=float)

        if h == 0:  # 基频没有PD特征
            return pd_features

        # print("harmonic_indices", harmonic_indices)
        for frame in range(0, n_frames):

            k_h = harmonic_indices[h, frame]
            k_h_prev = harmonic_indices[h-1, frame]
            k_0 = harmonic_indices[0, frame]

            pd = phase[k_h, frame] - phase[k_h_prev, frame] - phase[k_0, frame]
            pd_features[frame] = pd
            # 关键归一化步骤
            # 等价于：pd = (pd + π) % (2π) - π
            pd = np.angle(np.exp(1j * pd))

            pd_features[frame] = pd
        return pd_features

    def _temporal_average_pd(self, pd_features, h):
        _, n_frames = pd_features.shape
        pd_avg = np.zeros(n_frames, dtype=float)
        if h == 0:
            return pd_avg
        for frame in range(0, n_frames):
            forward_frame = frame + self.delta_forward + 1
            backward_frame = frame - self.delta_backward
            if forward_frame > n_frames:
                forward_frame = n_frames
            if backward_frame < 0:
                backward_frame = 0
            window_pd = pd_features[h,backward_frame: forward_frame]

            complex_sum = 0j
            for pd_val in window_pd:
                complex_sum += np.exp(1j * pd_val)

            # 计算平均相位
            if np.abs(complex_sum) > 0:
                pd_avg[frame] = np.angle(complex_sum)
            else:
                pd_avg[frame] = float('nan')
                print("error!! pd_avg can't calculate")
        return pd_avg


    def stft_with_explicit_padding(self, signal):
        """
        手动补零的STFT
        """
        """
        手动补零的STFT，模拟Rust的62帧处理
        """
        frame_length = self.frame_length
        hop_length = self.hop_length

        # Rust处理了前15872个样本，丢弃最后128个
        n_samples_to_process = len(signal) - (len(signal) % hop_length)
        if n_samples_to_process == 0:
            n_samples_to_process = len(signal)

        # Rust实际处理的音频样本数
        processed_audio = signal[:n_samples_to_process]

        # 在开头添加256个零（模拟Rust的analysis_mem初始缓存）
        padded_signal = np.pad(processed_audio, (frame_length - hop_length, 0), mode='constant')
        return  padded_signal


    def stft_with_explicit_padding_torch(self, signal):
        """
        手动补零的STFT - PyTorch版本
        Args:
            signal: Tensor, 形状为 [B, samples]
        Returns:
            padded_signal: Tensor, 形状为 [B, padded_samples]
        """
        frame_length = self.frame_length
        hop_length = self.hop_length

        B, n_samples = signal.shape

        # Rust处理了前n_samples_to_process个样本，丢弃最后不足hop_length的样本
        n_samples_to_process = n_samples - (n_samples % hop_length)
        if n_samples_to_process == 0:
            n_samples_to_process = n_samples

        # Rust实际处理的音频样本数
        processed_audio = signal[:, :n_samples_to_process]  # [B, n_samples_to_process]
        print("processed_audio shape", processed_audio.shape)
        # 在开头添加(frame_length - hop_length)个零（模拟Rust的analysis_mem初始缓存）
        # 使用F.pad进行填充，格式为(左边填充, 右边填充, 上边填充, 下边填充, ...)
        # 对于1D信号，格式为(左边填充, 右边填充)
        # 对于2D张量[B, T]，我们需要在时间维度(第二个维度)的左侧填充
        pad_left = frame_length - hop_length
        padded_signal = F.pad(
            processed_audio,
            (pad_left, 0),  # 左边填充pad_left，右边填充0
            mode='constant',
            value=0.0
        )
        return padded_signal
    def reconstruct_phase_torch(self, enh_magnitude, enh_phase, f0, noisy_magnitude):
        #magnitude [F, T]
        #phaseF, [F, T]
        #f0 [62]
        n_freq, n_frames = enh_magnitude.shape
        reconstructed_phase = enh_phase.copy()  #深拷贝



        # print("f0_bins ", f0_bins)
        # 获取谐波索引
        harmonic_indices, valid_harmonics = self._get_harmonic_indices(f0) #[max_harmonic, T]

        # 存储PD特征
        pd_features = np.zeros((harmonic_indices.shape), dtype=float)
        pd_avg_features = np.zeros((harmonic_indices.shape), dtype=complex)

        max_harmonic = harmonic_indices.shape[0]

        # 步骤1：计算所有谐波的PD特征
        for h in range(1, max_harmonic):
            if h == 0: #基频跳过
                continue
            pd_values = self._calc_phase_distortion(enh_phase, harmonic_indices, h)
            pd_features[h, :] = pd_values

        # 计算平均PD特征
        for h in range(1, max_harmonic):
            if h == 0:
                continue #基频跳过
            # 对PD进行时间平均
            pd_avg = self._temporal_average_pd(pd_features, h)
            pd_avg_features[h, :] = pd_avg


        # 5. 相位重建主循环
        modified_count = 0

        for t in range(n_frames):
            f_0 = f0[t]
            if f_0 <= 0:
                continue  # 跳过无效基频
            kappa_0 = f_0 * self.frame_length / self.sr
            delta_range = int(np.round(kappa_0 / 2))
            # print("delta_range ", delta_range)

            for h in range(max_harmonic):
                if not valid_harmonics[h, t]:
                    continue
                k_h = harmonic_indices[h, t]
                if k_h < 0 or k_h >= n_freq:
                    continue


				if h == 0 or h == 1:
					reconstructed_phase[k_h, t] = enh_phase[k_h, t]
				else:
					# 获取前一谐波和基频索引
					k_h_prev = harmonic_indices[h - 1, t]
					k_0 = harmonic_indices[0, t]

					if k_h_prev >= 0 and k_0 >= 0:
						# 应用公式(13)
						phase_h_prev = enh_phase[k_h_prev, t]
						phase_0 = enh_phase[k_0, t]
						phi_tilde = pd_avg_features[h, t]

						new_phase = phi_tilde + phase_h_prev + phase_0

						#归一化 等价于：pd = (pd + π) % (2π) - π
						new_phase = np.angle(np.exp(1j * new_phase))

						reconstructed_phase[k_h, t] = new_phase
						modified_count += 1

                if h > 0:  # 从第1谐波开始
                    k_h = harmonic_indices[h, t]
                    if k_h < 0:
                        continue

                    # 计算当前谐波的非整数索引kappa_h
                    f_h = (h + 1) * f_0
                    kappa_h = f_h * self.frame_length / self.sr

                    # δ的范围是基于基频的κ₀/2
                    for delta in range(-delta_range, delta_range + 1):
                        k_delta = k_h + delta

                        if k_delta < 0 or k_delta >= n_freq:
                            continue

                        # 跳过谐波本身
                        if delta == 0:
                            continue


						# 窗口相位补偿
						window_comp = self._compute_window_phase_compensation(
							k_h, kappa_h, delta
						)
						new_phase = (reconstructed_phase[k_h, t] +
									 window_comp)
						new_phase = np.angle(np.exp(1j * new_phase))

						reconstructed_phase[k_delta, t] = new_phase
						modified_count += 1
        print("modified_count", modified_count)

        return reconstructed_phase
    def spec_process(self, enh_stft, clean_stft, clean_wav, noisy_stft):

        B, C, T, F, _ = enh_stft.shape
        device = enh_stft.device
        enh_stft_complex = torch.view_as_complex(enh_stft.reshape(B, C, T, F, 2).contiguous())
        # clean_stft_complex = torch.view_as_complex(clean_stft.reshape(B, C, T, F, 2).contiguous())
        noisy_stft_complex = torch.view_as_complex(noisy_stft.reshape(B, C, T, F, 2).contiguous())

        enh_magnitude = torch.abs(enh_stft_complex)  # 振幅
        enh_magnitude = enh_magnitude.squeeze(1)
        enh_magnitude = enh_magnitude.permute(0, 2, 1)
        print("enh_magnitude shape", enh_magnitude.shape)

        enh_phase = torch.angle(enh_stft_complex)  # 相位
        enh_phase = enh_phase.squeeze(1)
        enh_phase = enh_phase.permute(0, 2, 1)

        noisy_magnitude = torch.abs(noisy_stft_complex)  # 振幅
        noisy_magnitude = noisy_magnitude.squeeze(1)
        noisy_magnitude = noisy_magnitude.permute(0, 2, 1)

        # 2. 使用clean_wav计算基频轨迹
        # 注意：funda_F.wav_Yin可能需要修改以支持PyTorch
        clean_wav = self.stft_with_explicit_padding_torch(clean_wav)
        # print("clean_wav shape", clean_wav.shape)
        f0_tracks = []
        for i in range(B):
            # 将clean_wav转换为numpy用于Yin算法
            # 如果Yin算法支持PyTorch，可以直接使用Tensor
            clean_wav_np = clean_wav[i].cpu().numpy()
            # print("clean_wav_np shape", clean_wav_np.shape)
            f0_track = self.funda_F.wav_Yin(clean_wav_np)  # 假设返回(T,)
            # print("f0_track shape",f0_track.shape)
            f0_tracks.append(f0_track)

        f0_track = np.stack(f0_tracks, axis=0)  # [B, T]
        f0_track = torch.from_numpy(f0_track).to(device)
        # print("f0_track ", f0_track.shape)
        print(f"基频轨迹形状: {f0_track.shape}, 范围: [{f0_track.min():.1f}, {f0_track.max():.1f}]Hz")
        reconstructed_phase = []
        for i in range(B):
            # 3. 相位重建
            enh_magnitude_np = enh_magnitude[i].cpu().numpy()
            enh_phase_np = enh_phase[i].cpu().numpy()
            f0_track_np = f0_track [i].cpu().numpy()

            noisy_magnitude_np = noisy_magnitude[i].cpu().numpy()
            rp = self.reconstruct_phase_torch(
                enh_magnitude_np,  # [B, C, T, F]
                enh_phase_np,  # [B, C, T, F]
                f0_track_np,  # [B, T]
                noisy_magnitude_np,
            )
            reconstructed_phase.append(rp)
        reconstructed_phase = np.stack(reconstructed_phase, axis=0)  # [B, T]
        reconstructed_phase = torch.from_numpy(reconstructed_phase).to(device)
        outputs_stft_complex = enh_magnitude * torch.exp(1j * reconstructed_phase)
        outputs_stft_complex = outputs_stft_complex.permute(0, 2, 1)
        outputs_stft_complex = outputs_stft_complex.unsqueeze(1)
        outputs_stft = torch.view_as_real(outputs_stft_complex.contiguous())
        return outputs_stft_complex


class PDPhaseReconstructionTorch(nn.Module):
    """
    PyTorch版本的相位重建模块
    支持批量处理和GPU加速
    """

    def __init__(self,
                 sr: int = 16000,
                 fmin: float = 80,
                 fmax: float = 400,
                 frame_length: int = 512,
                 hop_length: int = 256,
                 win_type: str = 'hann',
                 device: torch.device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')):
        super().__init__()

        self.sr = sr
        self.fmin = fmin
        self.fmax = fmax
        self.frame_length = frame_length
        self.hop_length = hop_length
        self.device = device

        # 窗口函数
        if win_type == 'hann':
            window = torch.hann_window(frame_length, periodic=True, device=device)
        else:
            window = torch.ones(frame_length, device=device)
        self.register_buffer('window', window)

        # 预计算常数
        self.register_buffer('pi', torch.tensor(np.pi, device=device))
        self.register_buffer('two_pi', torch.tensor(2 * np.pi, device=device))

        # 预计算窗函数的相位响应
        self._precompute_window_phase()

        # 基频预测模块 (占位符，后续实现)
        self.f0_predictor = None

    def _precompute_window_phase(self, n_fft: int = 10240):
        """预计算窗函数的相位响应"""
        # 零填充窗口
        window_padded = torch.zeros(n_fft, device=self.device)
        window_padded[:self.frame_length] = self.window

        # 计算FFT
        window_fft = torch.fft.fft(window_padded)

        # 计算freqs和相位
        freqs_full = torch.fft.fftfreq(n_fft, 1 / self.sr, device=self.device)

        # 重新排序
        window_fft_shifted = torch.fft.fftshift(window_fft)
        window_phase_shifted = torch.angle(window_fft_shifted)
        freqs_shifted = torch.fft.fftshift(freqs_full)

        self.register_buffer('freqs_shifted', freqs_shifted)
        self.register_buffer('window_phase_shifted', window_phase_shifted)

    def _get_window_phase_at_frequency(self, freq: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        获取窗函数在指定频率处的相位 (向量化版本)
        freq: 频率张量，形状任意
        """
        # 频率折叠
        max_freq = self.sr / 2
        freq = ((freq + self.sr / 2) % self.sr) - self.sr

        # 扩展维度用于广播
        freqs_shifted = self.freqs_shifted.unsqueeze(0)  # [1, n_fft]
        freq = freq.unsqueeze(-1)  # [..., 1]

        # 找到最近的频率索引
        idx = torch.searchsorted(freqs_shifted, freq)  # [..., 1]

        # 处理边界
        idx = torch.clamp(idx, 1, len(self.freqs_shifted) - 1)

        # 获取插值点
        x0 = self.freqs_shifted[idx - 1]  # [..., 1]
        x1 = self.freqs_shifted[idx]  # [..., 1]
        y0 = self.window_phase_shifted[idx - 1]  # [..., 1]
        y1 = self.window_phase_shifted[idx]  # [..., 1]

        # 计算插值权重
        t = torch.where(x1 != x0, (freq - x0) / (x1 - x0), torch.zeros_like(freq))

        # 复数域插值
        complex0 = torch.exp(1j * y0)
        complex1 = torch.exp(1j * y1)
        complex_interp = (1 - t) * complex0 + t * complex1

        return torch.angle(complex_interp).squeeze(-1)

    def _compute_window_phase_compensation(self, k_h: torch.Tensor, kappa_h: torch.Tensor,
                                           delta: int) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        批量计算窗口相位补偿
        k_h: 谐波的整数频点索引 [batch, n_harmonics, n_frames]
        kappa_h: 谐波的非整数频率索引 [batch, n_harmonics, n_frames]
        delta: 整数偏移
        """
        # 计算频率偏移
        delta_freq1 = (k_h - kappa_h) * self.sr / self.frame_length
        delta_freq2 = (k_h - kappa_h + delta) * self.sr / self.frame_length

        # 获取窗函数相位
        phi_w1 = self._get_window_phase_at_frequency(delta_freq1)
        phi_w2 = self._get_window_phase_at_frequency(delta_freq2)

        # 相位差
        phase_diff = phi_w2 - phi_w1

        return torch.angle(torch.exp(1j * phase_diff))

    def _get_harmonic_indices_batch(self, f0_track: torch.Tensor) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        批量计算谐波索引
        f0_track: [batch, n_frames]
        返回:
            harmonic_indices: [batch, max_harmonic, n_frames]
            valid_harmonics: [batch, max_harmonic, n_frames]
        """
        batch_size, n_frames = f0_track.shape

        # 计算最大谐波数
        f0_min = torch.clamp(f0_track.min(), 1.0)
        max_harmonic = int(4000 / f0_min.item())

        # 创建谐波索引
        harmonics = torch.arange(1, max_harmonic + 1, device=self.device).unsqueeze(-1)  # [max_harmonic, 1]

        # 扩展维度用于广播
        harmonics_expanded = harmonics.unsqueeze(0)  # [1, max_harmonic, 1]
        f0_expanded = f0_track.unsqueeze(1)  # [batch, 1, n_frames]

        # 计算谐波频率
        harmonic_freqs = harmonics_expanded * f0_expanded  # [batch, max_harmonic, n_frames]

        # 转换为频点索引
        harmonic_indices = torch.round(harmonic_freqs * self.frame_length / self.sr).long()

        # 标记有效谐波
        valid_harmonics = (harmonic_freqs > 0) & (harmonic_freqs < self.sr / 2)

        # 将无效索引设为-1
        harmonic_indices = harmonic_indices.where(valid_harmonics, torch.full_like(harmonic_indices, -1))

        return harmonic_indices, valid_harmonics

    def _calc_phase_distortion_batch(self, phase: torch.Tensor,
                                     harmonic_indices: torch.Tensor,
                                     h: int) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        批量计算相位失真
        phase: [batch, n_freq, n_frames]
        harmonic_indices: [batch, n_harmonics, n_frames]
        h: 谐波索引
        """
        batch_size, n_freq, n_frames = phase.shape

        if h == 0:
            return torch.zeros((batch_size, n_frames), device=self.device)

        # 收集相位值
        k_h = harmonic_indices[:, h, :]  # [batch, n_frames]
        k_h_prev = harmonic_indices[:, h - 1, :]
        k_0 = harmonic_indices[:, 0, :]

        # 创建索引
        batch_idx = torch.arange(batch_size, device=self.device).unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, n_frames)
        frame_idx = torch.arange(n_frames, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0).expand(batch_size, -1)

        # 获取相位值
        phase_h = phase[batch_idx, k_h, frame_idx]
        phase_h_prev = phase[batch_idx, k_h_prev, frame_idx]
        phase_0 = phase[batch_idx, k_0, frame_idx]

        # 计算PD
        pd_features = phase_h - phase_h_prev - phase_0

        # 归一化
        pd_features = torch.angle(torch.exp(1j * pd_features))

        return pd_features

    def _temporal_average_pd_batch(self, pd_features: torch.Tensor,
                                   delta_forward: int = 1,
                                   delta_backward: int = 1) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        批量时间平均PD特征
        pd_features: [batch, n_harmonics, n_frames]
        返回: [batch, n_harmonics, n_frames]
        """
        batch_size, n_harmonics, n_frames = pd_features.shape

        # 转换为复数
        pd_complex = torch.exp(1j * pd_features)  # [batch, n_harmonics, n_frames]

        # 创建滑动平均核
        kernel_size = delta_forward + delta_backward + 1
        kernel = torch.ones(kernel_size, device=self.device) / kernel_size

        # 扩展维度用于卷积
        pd_complex = pd_complex.permute(0, 1, 2)  # [batch, n_harmonics, n_frames]

        # 应用1D卷积进行滑动平均
        kernel = kernel.view(1, 1, kernel_size)
        pd_avg_complex = F.conv1d(
            pd_complex.real, kernel, padding=kernel_size // 2
        ) + 1j * F.conv1d(
            pd_complex.imag, kernel, padding=kernel_size // 2
        )

        # 计算平均相位
        pd_avg = torch.angle(pd_avg_complex)

        return pd_avg.permute(0, 1, 2)  # [batch, n_harmonics, n_frames]

    def _compute_base_f0(self, clean_magnitude: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        基频预测模块 (后续实现)
        这里暂时使用随机基频，实际训练时替换为预测模块
        clean_magnitude: [batch, n_freq, n_frames]
        返回: [batch, n_frames]
        """
        batch_size, n_freq, n_frames = clean_magnitude.shape

        # 这里先返回随机基频，实际使用时替换为预测网络
        f0 = torch.ones(batch_size, n_frames, device=self.device) * 200
        f0 += torch.randn_like(f0) * 20
        f0 = torch.clamp(f0, self.fmin, self.fmax)

        return f0

    def forward(self,
                enhanced_magnitude: torch.Tensor,
                enhanced_phase: torch.Tensor,
                clean_magnitude: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        相位重建主函数
        Args:
            enhanced_magnitude: 增强后的幅度谱 [batch, n_freq, n_frames]
            enhanced_phase: 增强后的相位谱 [batch, n_freq, n_frames]
            clean_magnitude: 干净信号的幅度谱（用于基频预测）[batch, n_freq, n_frames]
        Returns:
            reconstructed_phase: 重建后的相位谱 [batch, n_freq, n_frames]
        """
        batch_size, n_freq, n_frames = enhanced_magnitude.shape

        # 1. 获取基频轨迹
        if clean_magnitude is None:
            clean_magnitude = enhanced_magnitude

        f0_track = self._compute_base_f0(clean_magnitude)  # [batch, n_frames]

        # 2. 获取谐波索引
        harmonic_indices, valid_harmonics = self._get_harmonic_indices_batch(f0_track)
        n_harmonics = harmonic_indices.shape[1]

        # 3. 计算PD特征
        pd_features = []
        for h in range(n_harmonics):
            if h == 0:
                pd_features.append(torch.zeros((batch_size, n_frames), device=self.device))
            else:
                pd_h = self._calc_phase_distortion_batch(enhanced_phase, harmonic_indices, h)
                pd_features.append(pd_h)

        pd_features = torch.stack(pd_features, dim=1)  # [batch, n_harmonics, n_frames]

        # 4. 时间平均PD特征
        pd_avg = self._temporal_average_pd_batch(pd_features)

        # 5. 初始化重建相位
        reconstructed_phase = enhanced_phase.clone()

        # 6. 相位重建（向量化实现）
        for b in range(batch_size):
            for t in range(n_frames):
                f0 = f0_track[b, t]
                if f0 <= 0:
                    continue

                kappa_0 = f0 * self.frame_length / self.sr
                delta_range = int(torch.round(kappa_0 / 2).item())

                # 处理谐波重建
                for h in range(n_harmonics):
                    if not valid_harmonics[b, h, t]:
                        continue

                    k_h = harmonic_indices[b, h, t]
                    if k_h < 0 or k_h >= n_freq:
                        continue

                    if h == 0 or h == 1:
                        # 保留基频和第一谐波
                        continue
                    else:
                        # 获取前一谐波和基频索引
                        k_h_prev = harmonic_indices[b, h - 1, t]
                        k_0 = harmonic_indices[b, 0, t]

                        if k_h_prev >= 0 and k_0 >= 0:
                            # 公式(13)
                            phase_h_prev = enhanced_phase[b, k_h_prev, t]
                            phase_0 = enhanced_phase[b, k_0, t]
                            phi_tilde = pd_avg[b, h, t]

                            new_phase = phi_tilde + phase_h_prev + phase_0
                            new_phase = torch.angle(torch.exp(1j * new_phase))

                            reconstructed_phase[b, k_h, t] = new_phase

                # 处理谐波附近的频点
                for h in range(1, n_harmonics):  # 从第1谐波开始
                    if not valid_harmonics[b, h, t]:
                        continue

                    k_h = harmonic_indices[b, h, t]
                    if k_h < 0:
                        continue

                    # 计算当前谐波的非整数索引
                    f_h = (h + 1) * f0
                    kappa_h = f_h * self.frame_length / self.sr

                    # 批量计算delta范围
                    delta_range_tensor = torch.arange(-delta_range, delta_range + 1, device=self.device)

                    for delta in delta_range_tensor:
                        if delta == 0:
                            continue

                        k_delta = k_h + delta
                        if k_delta < 0 or k_delta >= n_freq:
                            continue

                        # 窗口相位补偿
                        window_comp = self._compute_window_phase_compensation(
                            torch.tensor([k_h], device=self.device),
                            torch.tensor([kappa_h], device=self.device),
                            delta.item()
                        )

                        new_phase = reconstructed_phase[b, k_h, t] + window_comp
                        new_phase = torch.angle(torch.exp(1j * new_phase))

                        reconstructed_phase[b, k_delta, t] = new_phase

        return reconstructed_phase

    # def reconstruct_harmonic_phase(self, phase: torch.Tensor, pd_avg: torch.Tensor,
    #                                harmonic_indices: torch.Tensor, valid_harmonics: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    #     """
    #     重建谐波相位
    #     Args:
    #         phase: [B, T, F]
    #         pd_avg: [B, H-1, T] 平均PD特征
    #         harmonic_indices: [B, H, T] 谐波索引
    #         valid_harmonics: [B, H, T] 有效掩码
    #     Returns:
    #         reconstructed_phase: [B, T, F]
    #     """
    #     B, T, F = phase.shape
    #     _, H, _ = harmonic_indices.shape
    #
    #     reconstructed_phase = phase.clone()
    #
    #     # 创建索引
    #     batch_idx = torch.arange(B, device=self.device).unsqueeze(1).expand(-1, T)  # [B, T]

    #         phase_h_prev = phase_harmonics[:, h - 1, :]  # [B, T]

    #         new_phase = phi_tilde + phase_h_prev + phase_0

    #         # print("new_phase shape", new_phase.shape)
    #         # print("new_phase", new_phase)

    #                 if valid_harmonics[b, h, t] and k_h[b, t] < F:
    #                     reconstructed_phase[b, t, k_h[b, t]] = new_phase[b, t]

    #     return reconstructed_phase
	
	
	
    # def reconstruct_surrounding_phase(self, phase: torch.Tensor, f0_track: torch.Tensor,

    #
